\end{equation*}
proving \cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-future-tail}.  Positivity preserves every
earlier crossing, so the unique delta at event $k$ is $w_k$.

The verified template makes the two scalar inequalities uniform over exactly
the stored future-label range.  Consequently an event counter plus the
checked fragment label/amplitude is sufficient to instantiate both the delta
and the universal future-safe certificate; no exterior response coordinate
is queried.  In the signed case the two checked exact template multiples
combine only after both stream inputs have been processed.  Counting the
declared scans, scalar checks, retained structural cells, and streamed replies
proves \cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-delta-eleven-vector} coordinate by
coordinate.  The final exclusions follow from the reporter's promised
template, event order, scale, and exact-real certificate.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[The scale reporter is invariant under verified permutations]
\label{thm:notched-sun-permutation-delta-reporter}
Keep every graph, cut, ladder, scale, gate, band, logical-column, and stream
hypothesis of \cref{thm:notched-sun-scale-delta-reporter}, but remove its
prescribed event order.  At interaction $t$, let all streams declare one
common label $\pi_t\in\{1,\ldots,n\}$ and supply the checked fragment on
$f_{\pi_t}$ with exactly the same nonnegative unit or signed $2$-minus-$1$
amplitudes as in that theorem.  Name the resulting online reporter
\textsc{NotchedSunPermutationDelta}.  It accepts the interaction exactly
when the declared petal belongs to the verified template, every stream uses
that petal and its required amplitude, and
\begin{equation}
 \pi_t\notin\mathsf{Seen}_{t-1},
 \qquad
 \mathsf{Seen}_t:=\mathsf{Seen}_{t-1}\cup\{\pi_t\},
 \qquad
 \mathsf{Seen}_0:=\varnothing.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-permutation-prefix}
\end{equation}
It rejects an invalid interaction before changing its state or emitting a
record.  Thus every accepted length-$n$ trace is a permutation, verified
online rather than promised in preprocessing.

Write
$y_q^{[t]}:=\sum_{s=1}^t h^{(\pi_s)}$ for each exact logical response, after
the verified signed combination when applicable.  For every accepted prefix
and every logical column $q$, the unique new delta at interaction $t$ is
$(q,w_{\pi_t})$, and the reporter emits one universal certificate with the
exact scalar payload
\begin{equation}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq \frac{9t\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta}
 \leq g-m
 \qquad
 \text{for every }i\notin\mathsf{Seen}_t.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-permutation-certificate}
\end{equation}
Its persistent state is
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf{PState}_t
 =(
   \text{verified template and label map},\text{fixed ladder},t,
   \mathsf{Seen}_t,\text{logical-column/sign metadata}),
 \label{eq:notched-sun-permutation-state}
\end{equation}
with no response entry, cumulative response coordinate, or exterior slack
cell.  A bit or dictionary entry for each template label implements
$\mathsf{Seen}_t$ and is already linear in the validated template size.

For $r$ nonnegative logical columns, $p=r$ and
$\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}=rn$.  For the separately checked signed
$2$-minus-$1$ streams, $p=2r$ and
$\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}=2rn$.  Processing the $p$ records of one
interaction sequentially and streaming the $r$ replies gives the complete
epoch ledger
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,n,\Theta(n+J+p),
 \Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+rn+n),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p),O(1),0,\Theta(rn)\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-permutation-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
Control charges every fragment-coordinate read, cross-stream label and
amplitude comparison, label-dictionary lookup, unused-bit test and update,
scalar-tail comparison, membership change, and reply construction.  The
extra permutation validation is $\Theta(pn+n)$ and is absorbed by the
displayed fragment/control and persistent-state coordinates.  There is no
response application, future-label scan, or numerical materialization.

This GO relaxes exactly the prescribed common event order of
\cref{thm:notched-sun-scale-delta-reporter}.  The face, exposed cut, label
pairing, complete ladder, fixed $n$, explicit near-one scale, gate and band,
one distinct unit logical fragment per petal, common cross-stream order, and
exact-real model remain fixed.  It does not cover repeated petals, missing
petals, different per-column orders, simultaneous fragment batches,
arbitrary amplitudes or fragments, template mutation, RPPR chronology,
fixed-$\alpha$ uniformity, a terminal solver, finite precision, or a general
reporter lower or upper bound.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The pointwise estimates in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-matching,eq:notched-sun-scale-future-tail} do not
use the numerical order of the petal labels.  At accepted interaction $t$,
entrywise positivity and the matching term give
\begin{equation*}
 y_{q,\pi_t}^{[t]}
 =\sum_{s<t}h_{\pi_t}^{(\pi_s)}+h_{\pi_t}^{(\pi_t)}
 \geq h_{\pi_t}^{(\pi_t)}>g.
\end{equation*}
If $i\notin\mathsf{Seen}_t$, then $i\neq\pi_s$ for every $s\leq t$.
Consequently every summand is off-matching, and the same operator-norm tail
used in \cref{thm:notched-sun-scale-delta-reporter} gives
\begin{equation*}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 =\sum_{s=1}^t h_i^{(\pi_s)}
 \leq\frac{9t\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta}
 \leq\frac12\vartheta^{5/2}=g-m.
\end{equation*}
Thus every unreported label was outside the band before the interaction,
only $w_{\pi_t}$ crosses now, and
\cref{eq:notched-sun-permutation-certificate} is available from the prefix
length and verified complement.  At $t=n$ the complement is empty and the
same certificate record is vacuously valid.

In the signed mode, checking both declared fragments first makes their exact
logical combination $2e_{f_{\pi_t}}-e_{f_{\pi_t}}=e_{f_{\pi_t}}$; no stream
read is credited away.  An array or dictionary indexed by the already
validated label map implements \cref{eq:notched-sun-permutation-prefix} with
one lookup and update per interaction.  Adding those operations to the
coordinate-by-coordinate ledger of
\cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-delta-eleven-vector} proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-permutation-eleven-vector}; none of its asymptotic
coordinates changes.  The rejection rule and final exclusions retain every
other promised-template axis.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Verified bounded repetitions need no response state]
\label{thm:notched-sun-multiplicity-delta-reporter}
Keep every hypothesis of
\cref{thm:notched-sun-permutation-delta-reporter} except the requirement that
each petal occur exactly once and the resulting $n$-event scale bound.  Replace
those two linked clauses by the following repetition contract and its exact
tail scale.  Before the epoch, declare and validate an exact multiplicity
vector
\begin{equation}
 \boldsymbol\mu=(\mu_1,\ldots,\mu_n)\in\mathbb N_{\geq1}^{n},
 \qquad
 L:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mu_i,
 \qquad
 H:=L-\min_i\mu_i.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-contract}
\end{equation}
Thus $L$ is the authoritative total number of accepted interactions, while
the entries $\mu_i$ are exact final multiplicities and online per-label caps.
In particular, a standalone maximum multiplicity is not used as a surrogate
for the total length.  Impose the exact scalar-envelope scale condition
\begin{equation}
 0<\vartheta
 \leq
 \frac{1}{\bigl(\sqrt{81H^2+3}+9H\bigr)^2},
 \quad\text{equivalently}\quad
 18H\sqrt\vartheta+3\vartheta\leq1.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-scale}
\end{equation}

Name the reporter \textsc{NotchedSunMultiplicityDelta}.  At interaction $t$
all streams must still name one common verified petal $\pi_t$ and carry the
same checked nonnegative-unit or signed $2$-minus-$1$ amplitudes as before.
With
\begin{equation}
 c_i(t):=\#\{s\leq t:\pi_s=i\},
 \qquad
 \mathsf{Seen}_t:=\{i:c_i(t)>0\},
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-prefix}
\end{equation}
the reporter accepts only if $t\leq L$ and
$c_{\pi_t}(t-1)<\mu_{\pi_t}$.  It checks the interaction completely before
incrementing the counter or emitting anything.  Hence invalid labels,
cross-stream label or amplitude disagreements, exhausted multiplicities, and
events after $L$ are rejected atomically.  Every accepted length-$L$ trace
automatically satisfies $c_i(L)=\mu_i$ for all $i$, so it is a permutation of
the declared multiset and no petal is missing.

At the first occurrence of $j$, the reporter emits the unique new delta
$(q,w_j)$ for every logical column $q$.  At a repeated occurrence it emits no
delta: $w_j$ was already reported, and no unseen label crosses.  After every
accepted interaction it emits one universal future-safe certificate per
logical column.  If the unseen complement is nonempty, its exact scalar
payload is
\begin{equation}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq\frac{9t\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta}
 \leq\frac{9H\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta}
 \leq g-m
 \qquad(i\notin\mathsf{Seen}_t).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-certificate}
\end{equation}
If the complement is empty, the certificate instead records that verified
fact and the scalar comparison is unnecessary.  Thus already-reported
matching labels are never incorrectly subjected to the unseen-tail bound;
their later matching repeats may only increase their exact responses.

The persistent state is
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf{MState}_t
 =\left(
  \begin{gathered}
   \text{verified template and label map},\text{fixed ladder},t,\\
   \boldsymbol\mu,(c_i(t))_{i=1}^{n},
   \text{logical-column/sign metadata}
  \end{gathered}
 \right),
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-state}
\end{equation}
where $\mathsf{Seen}_t$ is the predicate $c_i(t)>0$.  Replacing one seen bit
by one exact-cell counter per template label does not change the linear state
order.  No response entry, cumulative response coordinate, or slack cell is
stored.

For $r$ nonnegative logical columns, $p=r$ and every one of the $L$
interactions supplies one coordinate in each stream.  For signed logical
columns the positive and negative streams are separately read and checked,
so $p=2r$.  Respectively,
\begin{equation}
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}=pL=rL
 \qquad\text{or}\qquad
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}=pL=2rL.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-fragments}
\end{equation}
Streaming the $p$ records and $r$ replies gives the complete epoch ledger
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,L,\Theta(n+J+p),
 \Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+rL+n),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p),O(1),0,\Theta(rL)\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
Preprocessing charges validation and summation of all $n$ multiplicities.
Control charges all fragment reads, cross-stream comparisons, total-event and
per-label-cap tests, counter and seen-status updates, scalar comparisons,
first-occurrence tests, and reply construction.  Emission consists of $rL$
certificate records and exactly $rn$ delta records; since $L\geq n$, this is
$\Theta(rL)$.  There is no response application, future-label scan, response
state, slack materialization, or numerical materialization.

This GO relaxes exactly the no-repetition axis.  The frozen face, cut, label
pairing, complete ladder, fixed $n$, unit logical amplitudes, one common
cross-stream schedule, exact declared multiset, explicit multiplicity-
dependent near-one scale, and exact-real model remain promised.  It does not
cover unbounded repetitions, omitted petals in a completed epoch, arbitrary
amplitudes or fragments, batches, different per-column orders, template
mutation, RPPR chronology, fixed-$\alpha$ uniformity as $L$ grows, a terminal
solver, or coefficient-bit, word-RAM, rounding, or finite-precision costs.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The Neumann matching and off-matching estimates in
\cref{thm:notched-sun-scale-delta-reporter} are pointwise and tolerate
repetition.  If $i\notin\mathsf{Seen}_t$, then every one of the first $t$
events is off-matching for $i$.  Moreover the online caps imply
\begin{equation*}
 t=\sum_{j\ne i}c_j(t)
 \leq\sum_{j\ne i}\mu_j
 =L-\mu_i\leq H.
\end{equation*}
Summing the pointwise bound
$h_i^{(j)}\leq9\vartheta^3/(1-3\vartheta)$ therefore gives the first two
inequalities in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-certificate}.  Writing
$x=\sqrt\vartheta$, the last one is exactly
\begin{equation*}
 \frac{9H\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta}
 \leq\frac12\vartheta^{5/2}
 \quad\Longleftrightarrow\quad
 3x^2+18Hx\leq1.
\end{equation*}
The positive root is
$x=(\sqrt{81H^2+3}+9H)^{-1}$, which proves that
\cref{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-scale} is the unrelaxed, and hence sharp,
condition for this uniform scalar Neumann envelope.  This sharpness statement
is about the displayed envelope, not a lower bound on every possible
reporter.

Because $n\geq5$ and every $\mu_i\geq1$, $H\geq4$.  The scale condition gives
$\sqrt\vartheta<1/(18H)\leq1/72<10/51$.  Together with $d_{u_i}\leq5$ and
$c_\eta<1/50$, the same length-one Neumann term as in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-matching} is strictly greater than
$g=\vartheta^{5/2}$.  Immediately before the first occurrence of $j$, its
response contains only off-matching terms and is at most $g-m$; adding the
positive matching term puts it strictly above $g$.  Every other unseen label
continues to satisfy
\cref{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-certificate}, so precisely $w_j$ is new.
On a repeat, the matching label is already in $\mathsf{Seen}$ and all labels
outside $\mathsf{Seen}$ still satisfy the same certificate, so no delta is
new.  Once the complement is empty, the certificate is vacuous regardless of
the remaining repeats.

The signed streams again combine only after both exact fragments pass their
common-label, amplitude, and multiplicity checks.  The count vector both
implements seen membership and makes exhaustion atomic.  Finally, summing
the declared template scan, ladder and multiplicity preprocessing, $pL$
fragment reads, $rL$ certificates, $rn$ first-occurrence deltas, and
linear-size persistent cells proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-multiplicity-eleven-vector} coordinate by coordinate.
The state and scope claims follow directly from the named interface.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Declared positive amplitudes have a mass-capacity reporter]
\label{thm:notched-sun-amplitude-delta-reporter}
Keep the frozen face, exposed cut, label pairing, complete ladder, fixed
$n\geq5$, gate $g=\vartheta^{5/2}$, exact-real model, and common
cross-stream label order of
\cref{thm:notched-sun-multiplicity-delta-reporter}.  Replace only its unit
logical amplitudes, and consequently its unit-specific half-gate band and
event-count scale, by the following declared common amplitude contract.
For every label $i$, declare one occurrence-indexed schedule
\begin{equation}
 \mu_i\in\mathbb N_{\geq1},
 \qquad
 a_{i,1},\ldots,a_{i,\mu_i}\in\mathbb R_{>0},
 \qquad
 A_{i,k}:=\sum_{s=1}^{k}a_{i,s},
 \qquad A_{i,0}:=0.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-schedule}
\end{equation}
The same schedule is used by every logical column.  Put
\begin{equation}
 L:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mu_i,
 \qquad A_i:=A_{i,\mu_i},
 \qquad \mathcal A:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}A_i.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-total-mass}
\end{equation}
Thus $L$ is the total event count, $A_i$ is the final logical mass on label
$i$, and $\mathcal A$ is the total positive logical amplitude mass.  These
are distinct from the exact-cell fragment count below.

Assume $0<\vartheta<1/3$ and define the exact scalar matching coefficient,
Neumann remainder envelope, and response-mass capacity
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_i:=\frac{\vartheta^2}{d_{u_i}(1+c_\eta)},
 \qquad
 \epsilon_\vartheta:=\frac{9\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta},
 \qquad
 \kappa_i:=\frac{g}{\lambda_i}
 =d_{u_i}(1+c_\eta)\sqrt\vartheta.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-capacity}
\end{equation}
The first \emph{certified} occurrence index is
\begin{equation}
 k_i^*:=\min\{k\in\{1,\ldots,\mu_i\}:A_{i,k}>\kappa_i\}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-crossing-index}
\end{equation}
Require this set to be nonempty for every $i$.  Do not identify $k_i^*$ with
one: small first amplitudes are legal.  Define its positive pre-crossing mass
gap, and the maximum event count and logical mass that can have arrived while
label $i$ is still unreported, by
\begin{align}
 \gamma_i
 &:=\kappa_i-A_{i,k_i^*-1}>0,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-capacity-gap}\\
 H_i
 &:=L-\mu_i+k_i^*-1,
 &
 F_i
 &:=\mathcal A-A_i+A_{i,k_i^*-1}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-future-capacities}
\end{align}
Finally impose the exact schedule-dependent scale/certificate test
\begin{equation}
 m_{\mathcal A}
 :=\min_{1\leq i\leq n}
   \{\lambda_i\gamma_i-\epsilon_\vartheta F_i\}>0.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-margin}
\end{equation}
It automatically gives $0<m_{\mathcal A}<g$, so use
$[g-m_{\mathcal A},g]$ as the declared transition band.  For every fixed
finite positive schedule, all of
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-crossing-index,eq:notched-sun-amplitude-margin}
hold for all sufficiently small positive $\vartheta$: then $k_i^*=1$,
$\lambda_i\gamma_i=g$, and
$\epsilon_\vartheta F_i/g\to0$.  The theorem also covers validated scales at
which some $k_i^*>1$.

Name the resulting reporter \textsc{NotchedSunAmplitudeDelta}.  At
interaction $t$, all streams first name one common verified label $\pi_t$.
If $c_i(t-1)$ is the number of earlier accepted occurrences of $i$, the
required logical amplitude is
\begin{equation}
 a_t:=a_{\pi_t,c_{\pi_t}(t-1)+1}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-event}
\end{equation}
The reporter atomically checks the common label, the exact required stream
amplitudes, the per-label cap $c_{\pi_t}(t-1)<\mu_{\pi_t}$, and the total
cap $t\leq L$ before changing state.  Let
\begin{equation}
 S_i(t):=A_{i,c_i(t)},
 \qquad
 \mathcal A(t):=\sum_{i=1}^{n}S_i(t)=\sum_{s=1}^{t}a_s,
 \qquad
 \mathsf{Reported}_t:=\{i:c_i(t)\geq k_i^*\}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-prefix-state}
\end{equation}
Every accepted length-$L$ trace has $c_i(L)=\mu_i$ for every $i$, because
the counts are capped coordinatewise and their sum is $L=\sum_i\mu_i$.
Unseen labels have $c_i(t)=0$; seen but unreported labels have
$0<c_i(t)<k_i^*$; later occurrences of a reported label have
$c_i(t)>k_i^*$.  These three cases are not conflated.

For each logical column $q$, let the exact combined logical response be
\begin{equation*}
 y_q^{[t]}:=\sum_{s=1}^{t}a_sh^{(\pi_s)}.
\end{equation*}
If interaction $t$ changes $c_j$ from $k_j^*-1$ to $k_j^*$, the reporter
emits the single new delta $(q,w_j)$.  At every other occurrence it emits no
delta.  After every accepted interaction it also emits one universal
certificate per logical column asserting
\begin{equation}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}\leq g-m_{\mathcal A}
 \qquad(i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_t).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-certificate}
\end{equation}
The certificate remains valid simultaneously for unseen and seen-but-
unreported labels and already accounts for the largest possible future mass
from every other label.  If every label has been reported, it records the
verified empty complement.  Consequently the event at which count $k_i^*$
arrives is both the first certified crossing and the first exact strict
crossing of $g$ by label $i$, for every accepted interleaving of the declared
occurrence schedules.

The reporter retains one common copy of the declared schedules or their
prefix arrays.  Before the epoch it reads every one of the $L$ exact
amplitude cells, verifies positivity and the multiplicities, constructs all
prefix sums, and rejects unless the capacities, crossing indices, horizons,
and margin above pass.  Its persistent state is
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf{AState}_t=\left(
 \begin{gathered}
  \text{verified template and label map},\text{fixed ladder},t,\mathcal A(t),\\
  (\mu_i),(A_{i,k}),(k_i^*),(c_i(t)),
  \text{logical-column/sign metadata}
 \end{gathered}
 \right).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-reporter-state}
\end{equation}
Reported membership is the count comparison in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-prefix-state}.  No response coordinate,
cumulative response coordinate, exterior slack, or future-label scan is
stored or performed.

For $r$ nonnegative logical columns, event $t$ supplies
$a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ in each of the $p=r$ streams.  For the existing signed
$2$-minus-$1$ interface, each logical column supplies
$2a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ and $a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ in separately checked positive
and negative streams, so $p=2r$ and their exact logical difference is again
$a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$.  Hence the exact-cell counts and total absolute supplied
coefficient masses are respectively
\begin{align}
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=pL=rL,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}&=r\mathcal A,
 &&\text{nonnegative},
 \notag\\
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=pL=2rL,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}&=3r\mathcal A,
 &&\text{signed $2$-minus-$1$}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-fragments}
\end{align}
No cancellation is credited before both signed fragments are read and
checked.

Preprocess the $L$ amplitude cells and their prefixes once, process the $p$
stream records sequentially, and stream the $r$ replies.  The complete epoch
vector is
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,L,\Theta(n+J+p+L),
 \Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+rL+L+n),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p+L),O(1),0,\Theta(rL)\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
The $\Theta(rL)$ emission contains one certificate per column and event plus
exactly $rn$ delta records.  Control charges every fragment read, exact
amplitude and cross-stream comparison, count/capacity update, first-crossing
test, membership change, and reply construction.  There is no factor
application, numerical recurrence, response-state write, or numerical
materialization.

This GO relaxes the unit-logical-amplitude clause to a finite declared
positive common occurrence schedule and recalibrates only the scale-derived
band needed by that relaxation.  It keeps the frozen face, cut, ladder,
pairing, fixed $n$, common cross-stream label interleaving, exact
multiplicities, complete label coverage, and exact-real arithmetic.  It does
not cover undeclared or unbounded amplitude mass, arbitrary signed logical
amplitudes beyond the checked $2$-minus-$1$ encoding, different per-column
schedules, batches, arbitrary fragment support, template mutation, RPPR
chronology, fixed-$\alpha$ uniformity, a terminal solver, or coefficient-bit,
word-RAM, rounding, or finite-precision costs.  Failure of
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-margin} only rejects this scalar-envelope
certificate; it is not a lower bound requiring a response query.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
The entrywise nonnegative Neumann series in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-scale-neumann-series} is valid when
$0<\vartheta<1/3$.  For a matching pair $(u_i,f_i)$, its length-one term and
the norm bound on all later terms give
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_i\leq h_i^{(i)}\leq\lambda_i+\epsilon_\vartheta.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-pointwise-matching}
\end{equation}
For $j\ne i$, the length-zero and length-one terms vanish, so
\begin{equation}
 0<h_i^{(j)}\leq\epsilon_\vartheta.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-pointwise-off}
\end{equation}
Linearity and positivity therefore imply, at every accepted prefix,
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_iS_i(t)
 \leq y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq \lambda_iS_i(t)+\epsilon_\vartheta\mathcal A(t).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-prefix-envelope}
\end{equation}

If $i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_t$, then $c_i(t)\leq k_i^*-1$.  Positivity of
the schedule and the other per-label caps give, without any assumption on
the interleaving,
\begin{equation*}
 S_i(t)\leq A_{i,k_i^*-1}=\kappa_i-\gamma_i,
 \qquad
 t\leq H_i,
 \qquad
 \mathcal A(t)\leq F_i.
\end{equation*}
Substitute these bounds into
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-prefix-envelope}:
\begin{equation*}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq\lambda_i(\kappa_i-\gamma_i)
      +\epsilon_\vartheta F_i
 =g-(\lambda_i\gamma_i-\epsilon_\vartheta F_i)
 \leq g-m_{\mathcal A}.
\end{equation*}
This proves the universal certificate for unseen and seen-but-unreported
labels, including all mass that may already have arrived from future events
in other label schedules.

When the $k_i^*$th occurrence of $i$ is accepted,
$S_i(t)=A_{i,k_i^*}>\kappa_i$.  The lower half of
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-prefix-envelope} gives
\begin{equation*}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}>\lambda_i\kappa_i=g.
\end{equation*}
Immediately before that event, the same bound puts the label at most
$g-m_{\mathcal A}$; for a first interaction this is simply the initialized
zero response.  Thus no small earlier occurrence or
off-matching future mass caused an unreported crossing, and precisely this
event is its first exact and certified crossing.  Other unreported labels
continue to satisfy the certificate.  Once a label is reported, positivity
may increase it further but creates no second delta.

All quantities on the right side of the certificate are computed by one
preprocessing pass over the declared schedules.  Online operation needs only
the current label's count, next amplitude, and the global prefix mass; the
proof, not an online scan, quantifies over the complement.  The signed
streams combine to the same positive logical increment only after their
separate exact checks.  Summing the structural scan, ladder, schedule cells,
fragment records, constant-cell count updates, certificates, and deltas
proves \cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-eleven-vector} coordinate by
coordinate.  The state and exclusions follow from the named interface.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Independent positive column schedules have an asynchronous
reporter]
\label{thm:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-delta-reporter}
Keep the frozen face, exposed cut, label pairing, complete ladder, fixed
$n\geq5$, gate $g=\vartheta^{5/2}$, exact-real model, positive logical
amplitudes, and the nonnegative or signed $2$-minus-$1$ stream interface of
\cref{thm:notched-sun-amplitude-delta-reporter}.  Relax exactly its common
logical-column schedule.  There are $r\geq1$ logical columns.  For every
column $q\in\{1,\ldots,r\}$ and label $i\in\{1,\ldots,n\}$, independently
declare
\begin{equation}
 \begin{gathered}
  \mu_{qi}\in\mathbb N_{\geq1},
  \qquad
  a_{qi1},\ldots,a_{qi\mu_{qi}}\in\mathbb R_{>0},\\
  A_{qi,k}:=\sum_{s=1}^{k}a_{qis},
  \qquad A_{qi,0}:=0,
  \qquad
  A_{qi}:=A_{qi,\mu_{qi}}.
 \end{gathered}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-schedule}
\end{equation}
Put
\begin{equation}
 L_q:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}\mu_{qi},
 \qquad
 \mathcal A_q:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}A_{qi},
 \qquad
 L_\Sigma:=\sum_{q=1}^{r}L_q,
 \qquad
 \mathcal A_\Sigma:=\sum_{q=1}^{r}\mathcal A_q.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-totals}
\end{equation}
Thus $L_\Sigma$ counts externally accepted logical events and
$\mathcal A_\Sigma$ is their total positive logical coefficient mass.  They
are not the fragment-cell and absolute physical masses charged below.

For $0<\vartheta<1/3$, retain the structural matching coefficient and
Neumann remainder envelope
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_i:=\frac{\vartheta^2}{d_{u_i}(1+c_\eta)},
 \qquad
 \epsilon_\vartheta:=\frac{9\vartheta^3}{1-3\vartheta},
 \qquad
 \kappa_{qi}:=\frac{g}{\lambda_i}
 =d_{u_i}(1+c_\eta)\sqrt\vartheta.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-capacity}
\end{equation}
The notation $\kappa_{qi}$ records which declared column--label cell is
tested; its numerical value is column-independent because the frozen response
operator is shared.  Define
\begin{equation}
 k_{qi}^*:=
 \min\{k\in\{1,\ldots,\mu_{qi}\}:A_{qi,k}>\kappa_{qi}\},
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-crossing-index}
\end{equation}
and require every displayed set to be nonempty.  The exact pre-crossing gap,
local event horizon, global asynchronous event horizon, and response-relevant
mass horizon are
\begin{align}
 \gamma_{qi}
 &:=\kappa_{qi}-A_{qi,k_{qi}^*-1}>0,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-gap}\\
 H_{qi}^{\rm loc}
 &:=L_q-\mu_{qi}+k_{qi}^*-1,
 &
 H_{qi}^{\rm async}
 &:=L_\Sigma-\mu_{qi}+k_{qi}^*-1
 =H_{qi}^{\rm loc}+L_\Sigma-L_q,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-event-horizons}\\
 F_{qi}
 &:=\mathcal A_q-A_{qi}+A_{qi,k_{qi}^*-1}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-mass-horizon}
\end{align}
The distinction is essential: events in other columns can consume the extra
$L_\Sigma-L_q$ global interactions, but they contribute no mass to column
$q$'s response.  Give every column its own margin
\begin{equation}
 m_q:=\min_{1\leq i\leq n}
 \{\lambda_i\gamma_{qi}-\epsilon_\vartheta F_{qi}\},
 \qquad
 m_\Sigma:=\min_{1\leq q\leq r}m_q>0.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-margin}
\end{equation}
Then $0<m_q<g$ and column $q$ uses its own transition band $[g-m_q,g]$.
For every fixed finite family of positive schedules, all crossing sets and
margins pass simultaneously at every sufficiently small positive
$\vartheta$: then every $k_{qi}^*=1$,
$\lambda_i\gamma_{qi}=g$, and
$\epsilon_\vartheta F_{qi}/g\to0$.

Name the reporter \textsc{NotchedSunColumnAmplitudeDelta}.  The external
stream is any asynchronous interleaving of the declared column events.  At
global interaction $t\in\{1,\ldots,L_\Sigma\}$, one logical-event header
first names a column $q_t$ and a label $\pi_t$.  With $c_{qi}(t-1)$ the
earlier accepted occurrence count in that cell, its required logical amplitude is
\begin{equation}
 a_t:=a_{q_t\pi_t,\,c_{q_t\pi_t}(t-1)+1}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-event}
\end{equation}
In the nonnegative interface, the event contains exactly the one fragment
$a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ on physical stream $q_t$.  In the signed interface, it
contains exactly the paired positive fragments
$2a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ and $a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ on the positive and negative
physical streams belonging to that same column.  The reporter atomically
checks the column, physical stream identity, paired labels and amplitudes,
cell cap $c_{q_t\pi_t}(t-1)<\mu_{q_t\pi_t}$, column cap, and global cap before
changing state.  No event requires a label or occurrence to agree with any
other column, and one event is not a batch.

For every prefix define
\begin{equation}
 \begin{gathered}
  \ell_q(t):=\sum_{i=1}^{n}c_{qi}(t),
  \qquad
  S_{qi}(t):=A_{qi,c_{qi}(t)},
  \qquad
  \mathcal A_q(t):=\sum_{i=1}^{n}S_{qi}(t),\\
  \mathsf{Reported}_{q,t}:=
  \{i:c_{qi}(t)\geq k_{qi}^*\},
  \qquad
  \sum_q\ell_q(t)=t.
 \end{gathered}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-prefix-state}
\end{equation}
Every accepted length-$L_\Sigma$ trace exhausts every declared cell: all
counts are coordinatewise capped and their sum equals the sum of those caps.
The exact logical response in column $q$ is
\begin{equation}
 y_q^{[t]}:=
 \sum_{\substack{1\leq s\leq t\\q_s=q}}a_sh^{(\pi_s)}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-response}
\end{equation}
When the event changes $c_{qi}$ from $k_{qi}^*-1$ to $k_{qi}^*$, the reporter
emits exactly the new delta $(q,w_i)$.  Every other occurrence emits no delta.
After an event in column $q_t$, it emits one updated universal certificate
\begin{equation}
 y_{q_t,i}^{[t]}\leq g-m_{q_t}
 \qquad(i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_{q_t,t}).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-certificate}
\end{equation}
Certificates for $q\ne q_t$ remain valid without re-emission because neither
$y_q$ nor its counts changed.  Hence occurrence $k_{qi}^*$ is the first exact
strict crossing of $g$ in cell $(q,i)$ for every global interleaving.  The
certificate includes unseen and seen-but-unreported labels; an empty
complement is recorded as such.

Before the epoch, the reporter reads all $L_\Sigma$ amplitude cells, validates
positivity and complete label coverage, constructs every prefix, and rejects
unless all capacities, crossing indices, horizons, and margins pass.  Its
persistent state is
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf{CAState}_t=\left(
 \begin{gathered}
  \text{verified template and label map},\text{fixed ladder},t,\\
  (L_q),(\mathcal A_q(t)),(m_q),(\mu_{qi}),(A_{qi,k}),
  (k_{qi}^*),(c_{qi}(t)),\\
  \text{logical-column and physical-stream metadata}
 \end{gathered}
 \right).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-reporter-state}
\end{equation}
All $rn$ counter, crossing, and margin-heading cells and all schedule prefixes
are charged.  Since every $\mu_{qi}\geq1$, $L_\Sigma\geq rn$.  There is no
response coordinate, cumulative response coordinate, exterior slack cell, or
future-label scan.

There are $p=r$ persistent physical stream identities in the nonnegative
interface and $p=2r$ in the signed interface, but an asynchronous event reads
only its affected column.  Consequently
\begin{align}
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=L_\Sigma,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=\mathcal A_\Sigma,
 &&\text{nonnegative},
 \notag\\
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=2L_\Sigma,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=3\mathcal A_\Sigma,
 &&\text{signed $2$-minus-$1$}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-fragments}
\end{align}
No cancellation is credited until both signed physical records pass their
atomic pairing check.  The complete epoch vector is
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,L_\Sigma,\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),\\
 &\Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+L_\Sigma+rn+n),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),O(1),0,
 \Theta(L_\Sigma+rn)\bigr)\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,L_\Sigma,\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),
 \Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+L_\Sigma),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),O(1),0,\Theta(L_\Sigma)\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
The first line deliberately exposes the $rn$ declared cell headings, all
$L_\Sigma$ event validations, one certificate per event, and exactly $rn$
delta records before using $L_\Sigma\geq rn$.  Control also charges column and
stream identifiers, every fragment read, signed-pair comparison, amplitude
comparison, cap/count update, membership test, and reply construction.
Preprocessing and persistent memory charge the template, ladder, $p$ stream
identities, $rn$ schedule headers, all $L_\Sigma$ amplitude/prefix cells, and
per-column margins.  There is no factor application, numerical recurrence,
response-state write, or numerical materialization.

This GO relaxes only the common logical-column occurrence schedule and its
lockstep all-column event interface.  It preserves the frozen graph, face,
cut, ladder, label pairing, fixed $n$, complete label coverage in every
column, declared finite positive mass, exact-real arithmetic, and the atomic
signed pairing within each column.  It does not cover undeclared or unbounded
mass, arbitrary signed logical amplitudes, a signed pair split across global
interactions, simultaneous batches, arbitrary fragment support, incomplete
labels, template or ladder mutation, RPPR chronology, fixed-$\alpha$
uniformity, a terminal solver, coefficient-bit complexity, word-RAM costs,
rounding, or finite precision.  Failure of any margin in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-margin} rejects only this declared
scalar-envelope reporter; it is not a lower bound on response queries or
other reporters.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Fix one column $q$.  By
\cref{eq:notched-sun-amplitude-pointwise-matching,eq:notched-sun-amplitude-pointwise-off},
linearity and positivity give, for
every global prefix,
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_iS_{qi}(t)
 \leq y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq\lambda_iS_{qi}(t)+\epsilon_\vartheta\mathcal A_q(t).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-prefix-envelope}
\end{equation}
Only events with $q_s=q$ occur in this sum.  Thus asynchronous events in the
other $r-1$ columns affect global time but neither side of this envelope.

If $i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_{q,t}$, then
$c_{qi}(t)\leq k_{qi}^*-1$.  Positivity and the cell caps imply
\begin{equation*}
 S_{qi}(t)\leq A_{qi,k_{qi}^*-1}=\kappa_{qi}-\gamma_{qi},
 \qquad
 \ell_q(t)\leq H_{qi}^{\rm loc},
 \qquad
 t\leq H_{qi}^{\rm async},
 \qquad
 \mathcal A_q(t)\leq F_{qi}.
\end{equation*}
The event bounds are tight when all other permitted events are exhausted;
the response bound uses the local mass $F_{qi}$ rather than the generally
larger and irrelevant $\mathcal A_\Sigma$.  Substitution in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-prefix-envelope} gives
\begin{equation*}
 y_{q,i}^{[t]}
 \leq\lambda_i(\kappa_{qi}-\gamma_{qi})
      +\epsilon_\vartheta F_{qi}
 \leq g-m_q.
\end{equation*}
This proves the per-column certificate at every asynchronous prefix.

When occurrence $k_{qi}^*$ arrives,
$S_{qi}(t)=A_{qi,k_{qi}^*}>\kappa_{qi}$.  The lower envelope yields
$y_{q,i}^{[t]}>\lambda_i\kappa_{qi}=g$, while the preceding prefix was at
most $g-m_q$.  Every other unreported label in column $q$ retains its
certificate.  Every other column is exactly unchanged, so its last emitted
certificate remains valid.  Positivity prevents a second delta after a cell
is reported.  This proves exact first crossing and validates emitting only
the affected column's certificate.

The count caps and authoritative total $L_\Sigma=\sum_{q,i}\mu_{qi}$ prove
completion.  The paired signed fragments have exact logical difference
$a_te_{f_{\pi_t}}$ only after both positive records are validated.  Summing
the structural scan, schedule and prefix cells, physical records, atomic
checks, counters, certificates, and deltas gives
\cref{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-eleven-vector}; serial processing
uses constant temporary cells.  The stated state and scope follow from the
named interface.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Declared simultaneous batches preserve the column-local
reporter]
\label{thm:notched-sun-batched-columnwise-amplitude-delta-reporter}
Keep every hypothesis, schedule, capacity, and positive margin of
\cref{thm:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-delta-reporter}, but replace its
one-event interactions by atomic simultaneous batches.  A batch
$\mathcal B_h$, $h\in\{1,\ldots,B\}$, is a nonempty unordered collection of
tagged logical records $(q,i,k,a_{qik})$.  If $c_{qi}^{h-1}$ is the accepted
count before the batch, then, for some integer $b_{hqi}\geq0$, the records of
cell $(q,i)$ must have exactly the tagged amplitude records
\begin{equation}
 \left\{(k,a_{qik}):
 k=c_{qi}^{h-1}+1,\ldots,c_{qi}^{h-1}+b_{hqi}\right\}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-contract}
\end{equation}
The set in \cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-contract} is empty when $b_{hqi}=0$.
Thus a batch may contain several consecutive occurrences of one cell and may
mix arbitrary labels and columns, but it may not duplicate, replay, skip, or
invent an occurrence.  Put
\begin{equation}
 \begin{gathered}
  b_h:=\sum_{q,i}b_{hqi},
  \qquad
  s_h:=\left|\left\{q:\sum_i b_{hqi}>0\right\}\right|,
  \qquad
  b_{\max}:=\max_h b_h,\\
  S_\Sigma:=\sum_{h=1}^{B}s_h,
  \qquad
  \sum_{h=1}^{B}b_h=L_\Sigma,
  \qquad
  c_{qi}^{h}:=c_{qi}^{h-1}+b_{hqi}.
 \end{gathered}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-counts}
\end{equation}
Every batch is nonempty, so $B\leq S_\Sigma\leq L_\Sigma$ and
$b_{\max}\leq L_\Sigma$.

Name the reporter \textsc{NotchedSunBatchAmplitudeDelta}.  Before changing
any persistent state, it stages the whole current batch and atomically checks
its nonempty header, every column and label, every occurrence tag and exact
declared amplitude, the gap-free blocks in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-contract}, every cell/column/global cap, and every
physical stream identity.  In the nonnegative interface, logical record
$(q,i,k,a_{qik})$ contains exactly $a_{qik}e_{f_i}$ on stream $q$.  In the
signed interface it contains exactly the same-column, same-label, same-tag
pair $2a_{qik}e_{f_i}$ and $a_{qik}e_{f_i}$ on the positive and negative
streams.  A missing, split, mismatched, or cross-column pair rejects the whole
batch without changing a count or emitting a certificate.  No cancellation
is credited during validation.

At batch boundary $h$, define
\begin{equation}
 \begin{gathered}
  S_{qi}^{h}:=A_{qi,c_{qi}^{h}},
  \qquad
  \mathcal A_q^{h}:=\sum_i S_{qi}^{h},
  \qquad
  \mathsf{Reported}_{q,h}:=\{i:c_{qi}^{h}\geq k_{qi}^*\},\\
  y_q^{[h]}:=\sum_{i=1}^{n}S_{qi}^{h}h^{(i)},
  \qquad
  \mathsf{New}_{q,h}:=
  \{i:c_{qi}^{h-1}<k_{qi}^*\leq c_{qi}^{h}\}.
 \end{gathered}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-boundary-state}
\end{equation}
After a successful commit, it emits exactly the delta records
$(q,w_i)$ for $i\in\mathsf{New}_{q,h}$.  For each of the $s_h$ affected
columns, and only those columns, it emits one universal certificate
\begin{equation}
 y_{q,i}^{[h]}\leq g-m_q
 \qquad(i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_{q,h}).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-certificate}
\end{equation}
An unaffected column retains its preceding response, counts, and certificate
verbatim.  For every cell $(q,i)$, the unique batch whose endpoints satisfy
$c_{qi}^{h-1}<k_{qi}^*\leq c_{qi}^{h}$ is its first exact strict
\emph{batch-boundary} crossing of $g$.  No within-batch exact-crossing order
is defined or emitted, and no separate certificate semantics are assigned to
the simultaneous records inside a batch.

The reporter keeps the schedule state from
\cref{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-reporter-state}, replaces the event
counter by a batch counter, and stages at most one batch:
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf{BAState}_h=\left(
 \begin{gathered}
  \text{verified template, label map, ladder, schedules, and stream metadata},\\
  h,(\mathcal A_q^h),(m_q),(k_{qi}^*),(c_{qi}^{h}),
  \text{one atomic batch buffer}
 \end{gathered}
 \right).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-reporter-state}
\end{equation}
The persistent part has the same asymptotic size as before; the batch buffer
uses $O(b_{\max})$ exact cells, including the physical-pair staging by a
constant factor.  Batch contents and history are not retained after commit.

The logical event and physical fragment totals do not change under batching:
\begin{align}
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=L_\Sigma,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=\mathcal A_\Sigma,
 &&\text{nonnegative},
 \notag\\
 \mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=2L_\Sigma,
 &
 \mathcal A_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}
 &=3\mathcal A_\Sigma,
 &&\text{signed $2$-minus-$1$}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-fragments}
\end{align}
Here $p=r$ or $p=2r$, respectively.  There are exactly $S_\Sigma$
universal certificate records and exactly $rn$ delta records.  The complete
accepted-epoch vector is
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,B,\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),\\
 &\Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+L_\Sigma+B+S_\Sigma+rn+n),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),O(b_{\max}),0,
 \Theta(S_\Sigma+rn)\bigr)\\
 =\bigl(&\Theta(n),2,B,\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),
 \Theta(\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}+L_\Sigma),0,0,\\
 &\Theta(n+J+p+L_\Sigma),O(b_{\max}),0,
 \Theta(S_\Sigma+rn)\bigr).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
The unsimplified line exposes every batch header, logical occurrence and
physical fragment read, atomic validation, affected-column reply, and delta
construction.  The simplification uses
$B,S_\Sigma,rn,n\leq L_\Sigma$ and
$\mathcal C_{\rm frag}^{\Sigma}\geq L_\Sigma$.  An invalid attempted batch of
$\widehat b$ supplied logical records is charged one interaction, its staged
header/records and validation work, $O(1+\widehat b)$ temporary cells, and one
rejection record; it makes no persistent write.  Such attempts add to, rather
than disappear inside, the accepted-epoch vector.

This GO removes only the one-logical-event interaction restriction.  It keeps
the frozen graph, face, cut, ladder, pairing, fixed $n$, complete declared
positive schedules, per-column scalar margins, exact column separation,
exact-real arithmetic, and atomic within-record signed pairing.  It does not
cover a hidden chronology inside a simultaneous batch, undeclared or
unbounded mass, arbitrary signed logical amplitudes, a signed pair split
between records or batches, cross-column coupled response state, arbitrary
fragment support, incomplete labels, template or ladder mutation, RPPR
chronology, fixed-$\alpha$ uniformity, a terminal solver, coefficient-bit or
word-RAM complexity, rounding, or finite precision.  A failed margin still
rejects only this scalar-envelope reporter.
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
Fix a batch boundary $h$ and column $q$.  The batch response in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-boundary-state} is the sum of the same positive
logical fragments as any serialization of the accepted records.  Addition is
commutative, and the response column is linear, so
\cref{eq:notched-sun-columnwise-amplitude-prefix-envelope} applies at the
boundary without choosing an internal order:
\begin{equation}
 \lambda_iS_{qi}^{h}
 \leq y_{q,i}^{[h]}
 \leq\lambda_iS_{qi}^{h}+\epsilon_\vartheta\mathcal A_q^{h}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-batch-prefix-envelope}
\end{equation}
No record from another column occurs in this sum.

If $i\notin\mathsf{Reported}_{q,h}$, then
$c_{qi}^{h}\leq k_{qi}^*-1$.  The same coordinatewise caps and positivity as
in the one-event proof give
\begin{equation*}
 S_{qi}^{h}\leq A_{qi,k_{qi}^*-1}
 =\kappa_{qi}-\gamma_{qi},
 \qquad
 \mathcal A_q^{h}\leq F_{qi}.
\end{equation*}
Substitution in \cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-prefix-envelope} proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-certificate}.  Thus simultaneous mass on other
labels in the same column is already paid by $F_{qi}$, and mass in other
columns is absent because it changes a different response column.

If $i\in\mathsf{New}_{q,h}$, then before the batch
$c_{qi}^{h-1}\leq k_{qi}^*-1$, so the preceding certificate puts its exact
response at most $g-m_q$.  After the batch,
$S_{qi}^{h}\geq A_{qi,k_{qi}^*}>\kappa_{qi}$, and the lower envelope gives
$y_{q,i}^{[h]}>\lambda_i\kappa_{qi}=g$.  Conversely, every cell not in
$\mathsf{New}_{q,h}$ is either still certified below the band or was already
reported.  Hence the displayed delta set is exact, even when one batch jumps
over several occurrence indices or crosses several cells at once.  Positivity
prevents a second crossing.

The gap-free occurrence-tag check makes the post-batch counts independent of
record order and proves completion after $L_\Sigma$ logical records.  The
two signed physical fragments give the same positive logical increment only
after their same-record pairing check.  Staging before commit proves atomic
rejection and the $O(b_{\max})$ temporary-space charge.  Summing template and
schedule preprocessing, $B$ batch headers, all logical and physical records,
affected-column certificates, and the unique cell deltas proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-batch-eleven-vector} coordinate by coordinate.
\end{proof}

\paragraph{Cross-direction handoff.}
The reusable distinction is between global event latency and the state on
which a certificate depends.  Exact column separation lets unrelated events
increase $H_{qi}^{\rm async}$ without entering $F_{qi}$ or forcing a
recertification.  Atomic batching further shows that a positive linear state
needs only endpoint counts and total local mass; this says nothing about a
hidden within-batch chronology or about coupled state blocks.  An incremental
lower-state or reset-bank argument in another
direction may use the same accounting only after proving the analogous block
separation; a shared phase transition, response row, or reset reserve must
still pay for every component it actually changes.  In particular, an
event-local certificate or reset payment does not imply neighboring-stage
monotonicity or a generic scalar potential.  This observation adds no formal
dependency edge.

\begin{proposition}[Canonical singleton paths obstruct literal geometric
sleep]
\label{prop:singleton-path-geometric-sleep-obstruction}
Fix $\alpha\in(0,1)$ and $\gamma>0$.  For every sufficiently long unweighted
path $P_n=(v_0,\ldots,v_{n-1})$ with single seed $s=e_{v_0}$, form $D,Q,b$
using the \emph{ambient} degrees in $P_n$:
$d_{v_0}=d_{v_{n-1}}=1$ and $d_{v_i}=2$ for $0<i<n-1$.
There is a family-dependent $\rho_n>0$ for which the canonical
all-violations RPPR gate, initialized at $S_0=\{v_0\}$, has the forced trace
\begin{equation}
 S_k=\{v_0,\ldots,v_k\},
 \qquad
 B_k=\{v_{k+1}\},
 \qquad 0\leq k<n-1.
 \label{eq:singleton-path-gate-trace}
\end{equation}
Here, at the exact positive restricted point $x[S]$, the canonical batch is
\begin{equation}
 \mathcal B(S)
 :=\{v\in\partial S:
 c_v-Q_{vS}x[S]_S>0\},
 \qquad c:=b-\alpha\rho_nD^{1/2}\one,
 \label{eq:singleton-path-canonical-gate}
\end{equation}
so equality is inactive and every strict violation is admitted in the same
batch.  The proof chooses $\rho_n$ only after $n$ is fixed; no positive lower
bound uniform in $n$ is asserted.

For $1\leq k\leq n-3$,
\begin{equation}
 \frac{\operatorname{cvol}(S_{k+1})}
      {\operatorname{cvol}(S_k)}
 =1+\frac{3}{2+3k}.
 \label{eq:singleton-path-volume-ratio}
\end{equation}
Hence all but $O(1/\gamma)$ of the required gate certifications occur without
$\gamma$-multiplicative face growth.  A controller whose only certified
\textsc{BoundaryBounds} calls occur on a geometrically growing face sequence
cannot reproduce the literal gate on this family.

This does not give a work lower bound.  To make that exclusion fully charged,
use the non-prefetching append-only tridiagonal $LDL^T$ response written out
in the proof below.  It is the same response independently audited by the
stopping-time path materialization theorem in the companion
local-solver-oracle-hierarchy note (stable label
\texttt{thm:online-path-}\allowbreak\texttt{resource-separation}), now applied
to the append-only load $c_{S_k}$: old load entries retain their ambient
degrees and only the new coordinate is appended.  Request one
\textsc{BoundaryBounds} reply for each of the $n-1$ singleton batches and
co-return the empty-boundary terminal certificate and final vector at the
$n$th external stage.  The named exact-real execution has
\begin{equation}
 \begin{aligned}
 &(C_{\rm adj},R_{\rm adj},R_{\rm int},C_{\rm pre},C_{\rm ctl},C_{\rm rec},
 C_{\rm resp},M_{\rm pers},M_{\rm tmp},C_{\rm mat},C_{\rm emit})\\
 &\qquad=(\Theta(n),n,n,0,\Theta(n),0,\Theta(n),
           \Theta(n),O(1),0,\Theta(n)).
 \end{aligned}
 \label{eq:singleton-path-sleep-eleven-vector}
\end{equation}
This is an upper-bound realization, not a lower bound on its adjacency or
interaction rounds.  Terminal coordinates are final emissions, so they do
not enter intermediate $C_{\rm mat}$.  The proposition proves only that
\cref{cor:geometric-aggregate-debt-flush} cannot close online completeness by
itself.  A general theorem still needs an output-sensitive partial reporter
between checkpoints or a separately proved support-safe trace that bypasses
the literal canonical batches.  No finite-precision, word-RAM, bit-complexity,
or PPR-conversion claim is made.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
First set $\rho=0$.  For every prefix $S_k$, with all entries of $Q$ still
formed from the ambient $P_n$ degrees, the principal matrix
$Q_{S_kS_k}$ is an irreducible SPD Stieltjes matrix.  Its inverse is strictly
positive, while the restricted load $b_{S_k}$ is positive at $v_0$ and zero
elsewhere.  Therefore
\begin{equation*}
 x^{(k)}(0):=Q_{S_kS_k}^{-1}b_{S_k}>0.
\end{equation*}
The only boundary vertex is $v_{k+1}$, and its exact demand is
\begin{equation*}
 h_k(0)
 :=b_{v_{k+1}}-Q_{v_{k+1},S_k}x^{(k)}(0)
 =-Q_{v_{k+1},v_k}x^{(k)}_{v_k}(0)>0.
\end{equation*}
The active coordinates and the finitely many strict demands
\begin{equation*}
 h_k(\rho)
 :=c_{v_{k+1}}-Q_{v_{k+1},S_k}
 Q_{S_kS_k}^{-1}c_{S_k}
\end{equation*}
depend continuously (indeed affinely) on $\rho$.  Choose $\rho_n>0$ after
fixing $n$ so that every restricted prefix remains positive and every
$h_k(\rho_n)>0$.  This finite continuity choice supplies no $n$-uniform
lower bound.  Since the path prefix has exactly one boundary vertex,
canonical all-violations batching gives
\cref{eq:singleton-path-gate-trace}; the full restricted point is positive as
well.

The endpoint $v_0$ contributes $2$ to charged volume, and each
$v_i$, $1\leq i\leq k<n-1$, has full-graph degree two and contributes $3$.
Thus $\operatorname{cvol}(S_k)=2+3k$, proving
\cref{eq:singleton-path-volume-ratio}.  Once
$3/(2+3k)<\gamma$, the next required certification precedes
$\gamma$-multiplicative growth.  There are $n-O(1/\gamma)$ such indices.

Finally, write the tridiagonal factor of $Q_{S_kS_k}$ as
$L_k\Delta_kL_k^T$.  Starting with
$\Delta_0=Q_{v_0v_0}$ and $u_0=c_{v_0}$, admission of $v_{i+1}$ appends only
\begin{equation*}
 \ell_i:=\frac{Q_{v_{i+1},v_i}}{\Delta_i},
 \qquad
 \Delta_{i+1}:=Q_{v_{i+1},v_{i+1}}
   -\frac{Q_{v_{i+1},v_i}^2}{\Delta_i},
 \qquad
 u_{i+1}:=c_{v_{i+1}}-\ell_i u_i.
\end{equation*}
Put $w_i:=u_i/\Delta_i$.  At prefix $S_k$, back substitution starts with
$x[S_k]_{v_k}=w_k$, so the unique next demand is computed in constant work as
$c_{v_{k+1}}-Q_{v_{k+1},v_k}w_k$.  At termination, the one reverse response
application
\begin{equation*}
 x_{v_{n-1}}=w_{n-1},
 \qquad
 x_{v_i}=w_i-\ell_i x_{v_{i+1}}
 \quad(i=n-2,\ldots,0)
\end{equation*}
streams the terminal vector.  Thus appending the new ambient-degree row and
load entry costs constant response and control work.  Counting the $n$ row
exposures, $n$ external stages, retained records, scalar replies, and final
coordinates gives \cref{eq:singleton-path-sleep-eleven-vector}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Packed node tests with charged source whitening]
\label{cor:packed-harmonic-node-tests}
Suppose $\widetilde K_F$ satisfies
\cref{eq:frontier-spectral-approximation}, one multiplication by it costs
$T_{\rm mv}$ work, and one scalar measurement in
\cref{eq:chebyshev-whitened-group-estimator}, including construction or
maintenance of its target harmonic row, costs $T_{\rm harm}$ work.  Under
the hypotheses of \cref{lem:source-aware-hierarchical-locator}, all
potentially ambiguous leaves at one event are found with high probability in
\begin{equation}
 \widetilde O\!\left(
 \frac{T_{\rm mv}}{\sqrt\alpha}
 +
 T_{\rm harm}\,bL
 \left(1+|F|\frac{E^2}{m^2}\right)
 \right)
 \label{eq:packed-harmonic-query-work}
\end{equation}
work, plus exact reached-leaf validation and output.  No dense exterior
response or all-leaf squared range-add is used.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
Fix absolute constants $\xi,\zeta,\upsilon<1$.  The estimator
\cref{eq:chebyshev-whitened-one-sided} gives
\cref{eq:source-aware-group-oracle} with the absolute constant
$\gamma=\Gamma_{\xi,\zeta,\upsilon}$.  Apply
\cref{lem:source-aware-hierarchical-locator}; the
$O(\log^2(M/\delta))$ scalar measurements per reached group are absorbed by
$\widetilde O$.  The source vectors are prepared once and reused by every
reached group, so \cref{eq:frontier-whitening-work} is additive rather than
multiplied by the query count.
\end{proof}

\begin{theorem}[Low-cut-rank harmonic bank]
\label{thm:low-cut-rank-harmonic-bank}
Fix an anchor $A$, put $U:=V\setminus A$, and define its normalized cut
matrix
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf C_A:=Q_{AU}D_U^{-1/2}.
 \label{eq:normalized-anchor-cut}
\end{equation}
Suppose an exact rank-$s$ factorization
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf C_A=BC,
 \qquad
 B\in\mathbb R^{A\times s},
 \quad
 C\in\mathbb R^{s\times U}
 \label{eq:cut-rank-factorization}
\end{equation}
is available, and precompute the harmonic basis
\begin{equation}
 Y:=Q_{AA}^{-1}B.
 \label{eq:cut-rank-harmonic-basis}
\end{equation}
Then every target vector $a\in\mathbb R^U$ has harmonic extension
\begin{equation}
 Q_{AA}^{-1}Q_{AU}D_U^{-1/2}a=Y(Ca).
 \label{eq:cut-rank-target-extension}
\end{equation}
For every frontier $F\subseteq U$, $y\in\mathbb R^F$, and
$w:=Q_{AF}y$, its scalar transposed harmonic response is therefore
\begin{equation}
 a^TD_U^{-1/2}Q_{UA}Q_{AA}^{-1}w
 =(Ca)^TY^Tw.
 \label{eq:cut-rank-scalar-response}
\end{equation}

The low-rank factorization in fact removes target-side random measurements
entirely.  For $y\in\mathbb R^F$, define on $U$
\begin{equation*}
 b(y):=D_U^{-1/2}Q_{UF}y,
 \qquad
 t(y):=Y^TQ_{AF}y.
\end{equation*}
The normalized dual signature of the frontier lift is
\begin{equation}
 D_U^{-1/2}q_U=b(y)-C^Tt(y).
 \label{eq:cut-rank-dual-decomposition}
\end{equation}
For every hierarchy node $\mathcal C\subseteq U\setminus F$, store the
anchor-only Gram summary
\begin{equation}
 G_{\mathcal C}:=C_{:,\mathcal C}C_{:,\mathcal C}^T
 \label{eq:cut-rank-group-gram}
\end{equation}
and, for the current $y$, form the direct summaries
\begin{equation}
 r_{\mathcal C}(y):=C_{:,\mathcal C}b_{\mathcal C}(y),
 \qquad
 \nu_{\mathcal C}(y):=\|b_{\mathcal C}(y)\|_2^2.
 \label{eq:cut-rank-direct-summaries}
\end{equation}
Then the complete node response norm is the exact scalar
\begin{equation}
 \left\|D_{\mathcal C}^{-1/2}q_{\mathcal C}\right\|_2^2
 =\nu_{\mathcal C}(y)
  -2t(y)^Tr_{\mathcal C}(y)
  +t(y)^TG_{\mathcal C}t(y).
 \label{eq:cut-rank-exact-group-norm}
\end{equation}
All three group summaries are additive over hierarchy children.
$G_{\mathcal C}$ is built once from the cut columns.  The vector $b(y)$ is
formed by scanning frontier incidences; propagating each touched leaf to its
ancestors constructs every nonzero $r_{\mathcal C}(y)$ and
$\nu_{\mathcal C}(y)$.  If the hierarchy depth is $L$, this costs
$O(sL)$ per touched exterior label after the frontier scan.  Forming $t(y)$
reads $s$ entries of $Y$ per touched anchor row, and evaluating
\cref{eq:cut-rank-exact-group-norm} at a requested node costs $O(s^2)$.

Consequently, after the $s$ anchor solves and
$O(s|A|+s^2M)$ stored scalars for an $M$-node hierarchy, all fixed-frontier
group norms for one vector $y$ are obtained without a target harmonic solve,
a target Gaussian probe, or a scan of an untouched hierarchy subtree.
Polylogarithmic cut rank therefore closes the target side of
\cref{cor:packed-harmonic-node-tests}, conditional only on the declared
factorization, anchor-solve, and touched-incidence charges.

This rank dependence is exact.  The full harmonic bank
\begin{equation*}
 \mathsf H_A:=Q_{AA}^{-1}\mathsf C_A
\end{equation*}
has $\operatorname{rank}(\mathsf H_A)=
\operatorname{rank}(\mathsf C_A)$.  Moreover, for every integer $k\geq0$,
\begin{equation}
 \min_{\operatorname{rank}(\widetilde{\mathsf H})\leq k}
 \left\|Q_{AA}^{1/2}
   (\mathsf H_A-\widetilde{\mathsf H})\right\|_2
 =\sigma_{k+1}\!\left(Q_{AA}^{-1/2}\mathsf C_A\right).
 \label{eq:harmonic-bank-numerical-rank}
\end{equation}
An energy-operator error at most $\eta$ consequently guarantees, for all
$a$ and $w$,
\begin{equation}
 \left|a^T(\mathsf H_A-\widetilde{\mathsf H})^Tw\right|
 \leq\eta\|a\|_2\|w\|_{Q_{AA}^{-1}}.
 \label{eq:harmonic-bank-bilinear-error}
\end{equation}
The squared singular values have the general packing bound
\begin{equation}
 \sum_i\sigma_i^2\!\left(Q_{AA}^{-1/2}\mathsf C_A\right)
 =\sum_{v\in U}
   \frac{Q_{vA}Q_{AA}^{-1}Q_{Av}}{d_v}
 \leq |A|.
 \label{eq:harmonic-bank-singular-packing}
\end{equation}
\end{theorem}

\begin{proof}
\Cref{eq:cut-rank-target-extension,eq:cut-rank-scalar-response} follow by
substituting \cref{eq:cut-rank-factorization} and using symmetry.  The same
substitution in the exterior block
$q_U=Q_{UF}y-Q_{UA}Q_{AA}^{-1}Q_{AF}y$ proves
\cref{eq:cut-rank-dual-decomposition}.  Expanding the squared norm of
$b_{\mathcal C}(y)-C_{:,\mathcal C}^Tt(y)$ gives
\cref{eq:cut-rank-exact-group-norm}.  Each displayed summary is a sum of
leaf contributions, proving additivity.  Scanning the nonzeros of $b(y)$ and
the touched rows of $Q_{AF}y$, then propagating leaf contributions along
their ancestor paths, gives the work and storage statements.

Invertibility of $Q_{AA}$ preserves rank.  The energy-scaled approximation
problem is ordinary best rank-$k$ approximation of
$Q_{AA}^{1/2}\mathsf H_A=Q_{AA}^{-1/2}\mathsf C_A$; the
Eckart--Young theorem proves
\cref{eq:harmonic-bank-numerical-rank}.  Cauchy--Schwarz between
$Q_{AA}^{1/2}(\mathsf H_A-\widetilde{\mathsf H})a$ and
$Q_{AA}^{-1/2}w$ proves \cref{eq:harmonic-bank-bilinear-error}.  Finally,
expanding the Frobenius norm gives the equality in
\cref{eq:harmonic-bank-singular-packing}.  Apply
\cref{eq:source-aware-leverage-sum} with source set equal to the whole face
$A$, then use $d_v\geq1$, to obtain the inequality.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Low-cut-rank packed group reporter]
\label{cor:low-cut-rank-packed-reporter}
Assume the factorization and harmonic bank of
\cref{thm:low-cut-rank-harmonic-bank}, and let the boundary hierarchy have
depth $L$ and at most $M$ nodes.  Use the Chebyshev-whitened source vectors
$y_t=p_q(\widetilde K_F)g_t$ from
\cref{thm:chebyshev-frontier-whitening}, but replace the target-side Gaussian
estimator by
\begin{equation}
 \overline Z_{F,q}^{\rm lr}(\mathcal C)
 :=\frac1r\sum_{t=1}^r
 \left\|D_{\mathcal C}^{-1/2}q_{\mathcal C}(y_t)\right\|_2^2,
 \label{eq:low-rank-source-only-estimator}
\end{equation}
where every summand is evaluated exactly by
\cref{eq:cut-rank-exact-group-norm}.  For
$r=O(\xi^{-2}\log(2M/\delta))$, with probability at least $1-\delta$,
simultaneously for every node,
\begin{equation}
 (1-\xi)a_-Z_F(\mathcal C)
 \leq\overline Z_{F,q}^{\rm lr}(\mathcal C)
 \leq(1+\xi)a_+Z_F(\mathcal C),
 \label{eq:low-rank-source-only-guarantee}
\end{equation}
with $a_-,a_+$ from
\cref{eq:frontier-whitening-sandwich}.  Rescaling the estimator therefore
gives the one-sided group oracle required by
\cref{lem:source-aware-hierarchical-locator}.

Moreover, the factorization makes one exact frontier multiplication local.
Indeed,
\begin{equation}
 Q_{AA}^{-1}Q_{AF}y
 =Y\,C_{:,F}D_F^{1/2}y,
 \label{eq:low-rank-anchor-application}
\end{equation}
so \cref{eq:exact-frontier-matvec} costs
$O(s\operatorname{cvol}(F))$ row reads and arithmetic.  After the separately
charged factorization, $s$ anchor solves, and hierarchy-Gram construction,
one packed ambiguous-row search consequently costs
\begin{equation}
 \widetilde O\!\left(
  \frac{s\operatorname{cvol}(F)}{\sqrt\alpha}
  +s^2bL\left(1+|F|\frac{E^2}{m^2}\right)
 \right),
 \label{eq:low-rank-packed-reporter-work}
\end{equation}
plus exact reached-leaf validation and output.  Thus a polylogarithmic-rank
cut gives a complete charged group reporter at the accelerated frontier
scale; no target harmonic query remains.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
For fixed $\mathcal C$, the vector
$D_{\mathcal C}^{-1/2}q_{\mathcal C}(y_t)$ is the matrix
$R_{\mathcal C}p_q(\widetilde K_F)$ applied to a standard Gaussian source.
The first Gaussian quadratic-form argument in
\cref{lem:two-sided-harmonic-group-sketch}, followed by a union bound over
the $M$ nodes, compares
\cref{eq:low-rank-source-only-estimator} with
$Z_{F,q}(\mathcal C)$.  Combine this with
\cref{eq:frontier-whitening-sandwich} to prove
\cref{eq:low-rank-source-only-guarantee}.

The cut factorization gives
$Q_{AF}=BC_{:,F}D_F^{1/2}$, proving
\cref{eq:low-rank-anchor-application}.  The products incident on $F$ scan
its rows and read only the corresponding rows of $Y$.  Hence
$T_{\rm mv}=O(s\operatorname{cvol}(F))$ in
\cref{eq:frontier-whitening-work}.  For the logarithmic number of final
source probes, construct $b(y_t)$ from the same frontier incidences, propagate
\cref{eq:cut-rank-direct-summaries} along at most $L$ ancestors, and form
$t(y_t)$ from touched rows of $Y$; these costs are absorbed by the first term
up to logarithms.  Each queried group then costs $O(s^2)$ by
\cref{eq:cut-rank-exact-group-norm}.  Substitute the query count from
\cref{eq:source-aware-group-query-count} to obtain
\cref{eq:low-rank-packed-reporter-work}.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[A comb tree obstructs a universal low-rank bank]
\label{prop:comb-harmonic-rank-obstruction}
Fix $\alpha\in(0,1)$ and $n\geq2$.  Form an unweighted tree whose active
anchor is the path $A=\{u_1,\ldots,u_n\}$ and attach one exterior leaf $v_i$
to every $u_i$.  Put $U:=\{v_1,\ldots,v_n\}$ and
$q_{\rm off}:=(1-\alpha)/2$.  Then
\begin{equation}
 \mathsf C_A
 =-q_{\rm off}\operatorname{diag}
   \left(d_{u_1}^{-1/2},\ldots,d_{u_n}^{-1/2}\right),
 \label{eq:comb-cut-matrix}
\end{equation}
so the exact harmonic bank has rank $n$.  It is also entrywise nonzero.
Furthermore,
\begin{equation}
 \sigma_n\!\left(Q_{AA}^{-1/2}\mathsf C_A\right)
 \geq\frac{q_{\rm off}}{\sqrt3}.
 \label{eq:comb-harmonic-singular-lower-bound}
\end{equation}
Consequently every bank of rank below $n$ has energy-operator error at least
$q_{\rm off}/\sqrt3$, even though
$\operatorname{cvol}(A)=4n-2$.  Explicitly materializing all leaf harmonic
extensions stores $\Theta(n^2)$ nonzero values on a linear-volume tree.
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
Every $v_i$ has degree one and its only anchor neighbor is $u_i$, proving
\cref{eq:comb-cut-matrix}; the anchor degrees are at most three.  The
principal path matrix $Q_{AA}$ is irreducible Stieltjes, so
$Q_{AA}^{-1}$ is strictly positive.  Multiplication by the nonsingular
diagonal matrix in \cref{eq:comb-cut-matrix} proves both full rank and the
entrywise-nonzero claim.

Since $Q\preceq I$, also $Q_{AA}\preceq I$ and
$\sigma_{\min}(Q_{AA}^{-1/2})\geq1$.  The least diagonal magnitude in
\cref{eq:comb-cut-matrix} is at least $q_{\rm off}/\sqrt3$, which proves
\cref{eq:comb-harmonic-singular-lower-bound}.  Now apply
\cref{eq:harmonic-bank-numerical-rank}.  Finally, two path endpoints have
degree two and the other anchor vertices have degree three, so
$\operatorname{cvol}(A)=n+(3n-2)=4n-2$.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Full-rank exact losses in a nonequitable cyclic structural
epoch]
\label{prop:notched-double-sun-loss-rank}
Fix $n\geq5$.  Form an unweighted graph with anchor
$A=\{u_1,\ldots,u_n\}$ by taking the cycle
$u_1u_2\cdots u_nu_1$, adding the chord $u_1u_3$, and attaching two leaves
$f_i,w_i$ to every $u_i$.  Put
\begin{equation*}
 F:=\{f_1,\ldots,f_n\},
 \qquad
 W:=\{w_1,\ldots,w_n\},
 \qquad
 S_k:=A\cup\{f_1,\ldots,f_k\}.
\end{equation*}
The $f_i$ are frontier petals and the $w_i$ remain exterior report labels.
We prescribe the singleton face additions in the displayed order.  This is a
structural response-interface trace; no RPPR load, KKT gate, or unit-seed
instance generating that admission order is asserted.  For
$1\leq i,k\leq n$, define the exact normalized diagonal-loss increment
\begin{equation}
 z_i^{(k)}
 :=(Q/S_{k-1})_{w_iw_i}-(Q/S_k)_{w_iw_i}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-loss-increment}
\end{equation}
Then the following statements hold.

\begin{enumerate}[label=(\roman*)]
\item The anchor has
      $\operatorname{cvol}(A)=5n+2$.  The natural partition $(A,F,W)$ is
      not equitable, because $u_1,u_3$ have three anchor neighbors and the
      other anchor vertices have two.  The normalized anchor--petal cut has
      rank $n$.
\item For every $\alpha\in(0,1)$, every entry $z_i^{(k)}$ is strictly
      positive.  Hence each exact singleton loss update is dense on all $n$
      report labels.
\item For each fixed $n$ there is an $\alpha_n<1$ such that, whenever
      $\alpha\in(\alpha_n,1)$, the matrix
      \begin{equation*}
       \mathsf Z_n:=\bigl[z_i^{(k)}\bigr]_{i,k=1}^n
      \end{equation*}
      is nonsingular.  More precisely, with
      $\vartheta:=(1-\alpha)/2$ and
      $\delta_i:=d_{u_i}\in\{4,5\}$,
      \begin{align}
       z_k^{(k)}
       &=\frac{\vartheta^4}{\delta_k^2}
         +O_n(\vartheta^5),
       \label{eq:notched-sun-loss-diagonal}\\
       z_i^{(k)}&=O_n(\vartheta^6),
       \qquad i\neq k.
       \label{eq:notched-sun-loss-offdiagonal}
      \end{align}
      The first $K$ loss vectors are linearly independent for every
      $1\leq K\leq n$.
\item If the next checkpoint is scheduled at
      $\operatorname{cvol}(S_k)\geq
       (1+\gamma)\operatorname{cvol}(A)$ for fixed $\gamma>0$, then the
      number of singleton events through the next checkpoint or exhaustion
      is
      \begin{equation}
       K_\gamma
       =\min\!\left\{n,
          \left\lceil\frac{\gamma(5n+2)}2\right\rceil
        \right\}
       =\Theta_\gamma(n).
       \label{eq:notched-sun-light-epoch-length}
      \end{equation}
      Thus a prescribed charged-volume-$\Theta(n)$ structural epoch contains
      $\Theta(n)$ dense independent exact loss updates even though exposing
      each frontier petal costs only $\operatorname{cvol}(\{f_k\})=2$.
\end{enumerate}
\end{proposition}

\begin{proof}
Let $\mathsf D_A:=\operatorname{diag}(\delta_1,\ldots,\delta_n)$ and let
$N_A$ be the normalized adjacency of the cycle plus chord, using the degrees
in the full graph.  The two chord endpoints have degree five and every other
anchor vertex has degree four, so
\begin{equation*}
 \sum_{u\in A}(1+d_u)=n+(4n+2)=5n+2.
\end{equation*}
The nonequitability claim follows from the declared anchor-neighbor counts.
Since every petal has degree one, the normalized cut matrix, with the $F$
and $W$ columns in cyclic order, is
\begin{equation}
 Q_{A,F\cup W}D_{F\cup W}^{-1/2}
 =\begin{bmatrix}-C&-C\end{bmatrix},
 \qquad
 C:=\vartheta\mathsf D_A^{-1/2}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-cut}
\end{equation}
The diagonal matrix $C$ is nonsingular, proving cut rank $n$.

Put
\begin{equation}
 G:=Q_{AA}^{-1},
 \qquad
 H:=CGC,
 \qquad
 K:=(1-\vartheta)I-H.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-green-blocks}
\end{equation}
Eliminating the anchor gives, on the two petal copies,
\begin{equation}
 (Q/A)_{FF}=(Q/A)_{WW}=K,
 \qquad
 (Q/A)_{WF}=-H.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-petal-schur}
\end{equation}
The matrix $Q_{AA}$ is irreducible Stieltjes, hence $G$ is strictly positive
entrywise.  Thus $H$ is strictly positive.  The matrix $K$ is a principal
Schur complement of $Q$ and is Stieltjes.  For
$P_k:=\{1,\ldots,k-1\}$, one more Schur-complement step gives
\begin{align}
 r^{(k)}
 &:=- (Q/S_{k-1})_{Wf_k}
   =H_{:k}+H_{:P_k}K_{P_kP_k}^{-1}H_{P_kk}>0,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-conditional-response}\\
 \kappa_k
 &:=(Q/S_{k-1})_{f_kf_k}
   =K_{kk}-K_{kP_k}K_{P_kP_k}^{-1}K_{P_kk}>0.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-conditional-pivot}
\end{align}
Here an empty product is omitted when $k=1$.  Applying the scalar Schur
diagonal-loss identity yields
\begin{equation}
 z_i^{(k)}=\frac{(r_i^{(k)})^2}{\kappa_k}>0,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-loss-factor}
\end{equation}
which proves density.

It remains to prove independence.  As $\vartheta\downarrow0$,
\begin{equation*}
 Q_{AA}
 =(1-\vartheta)I-\vartheta N_A,
 \qquad
 G=I+O_n(\vartheta).
\end{equation*}
Consequently
\begin{align*}
 H_{ii}&=\frac{\vartheta^2}{\delta_i}
          +O_n(\vartheta^3),
 &H_{ik}&=O_n(\vartheta^3)\quad(i\neq k),
 &K_{P_kP_k}^{-1}&=I+O_n(\vartheta).
\end{align*}
Substitution in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-conditional-response,eq:notched-sun-conditional-pivot}
gives
\begin{align*}
 r_k^{(k)}
 &=\frac{\vartheta^2}{\delta_k}+O_n(\vartheta^3),
 &r_i^{(k)}&=O_n(\vartheta^3)\quad(i\neq k),
 &\kappa_k&=1+O_n(\vartheta).
\end{align*}
Equation~\eqref{eq:notched-sun-loss-factor} proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-loss-diagonal,eq:notched-sun-loss-offdiagonal}.  The
leading term of the determinant is therefore
\begin{equation*}
 \det(\mathsf Z_n)
 =\vartheta^{4n}\prod_{k=1}^n\delta_k^{-2}
  +O_n(\vartheta^{4n+1}),
\end{equation*}
which is nonzero for all sufficiently small positive $\vartheta$.  The same
argument on the first $K$ rows and columns proves independence of every
prefix.

Finally, every admitted source leaf adds two units of charged volume, so
$\operatorname{cvol}(S_k)=5n+2+2k$.  Solving the geometric checkpoint
inequality proves
\cref{eq:notched-sun-light-epoch-length}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[Exact fixed-linear target tags require linear dimension]
\label{cor:notched-sun-exact-tag-obstruction}
Use the first $K\leq n$ events of
\cref{prop:notched-double-sun-loss-rank} with
$\alpha\in(\alpha_n,1)$.  Suppose an epoch-internal target state lies in
$\mathbb R^s$, has a fixed linear decoder
$R:\mathbb R^s\to\mathbb R^W$, and represents every exact loss increment by
some state increment $a_k$, so that
\begin{equation}
 Ra_k=z^{(k)},
 \qquad 1\leq k\leq K.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-linear-tag}
\end{equation}
Then $s\geq K$.  Taking $K=K_\gamma$ from
\cref{eq:notched-sun-light-epoch-length}, for any fixed $\gamma>0$, gives
$s\geq K_\gamma=\Theta_\gamma(n)$.  Hence no
$s=\operatorname{polylog}(n)$ exact fixed-linear loss tag represents the
prescribed epoch.

The literal work ledger exposes two failed implementations.  Materializing
the dense vector $z^{(k)}$ at all leaves writes $n$ words per event and costs
$nK$ leaf updates before any hierarchy-node query, exact leaf validation, or
output.  Alternatively, every nonzero same-sign target vector on the petals
has a dense harmonic extension through $A$; returning one such extension as
an explicit old-face row writes $n$ words.  If $H_{\rm row}$ distinct rows are
constructed on demand, their construction alone costs at least
$nH_{\rm row}$ words.  Their later applications must additionally charge
every touched stored-row read; every reached hierarchy query, exact leaf
validation, and reported crossing remains a separate unit of work.

This is an exact-representation obstruction only.  The off-diagonal losses in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-loss-offdiagonal} can lie below a finite reporting
margin.  The result does not rule out the scale-matched additive oracle of
\cref{cor:additive-group-locator}, nor any scale-truncated, nonlinear,
adaptive, sparse-recovery, or cyclic transfer/separator representation.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The $K$ loss vectors are linearly independent and all lie in
$\operatorname{range}(R)$, so
$s\geq\operatorname{rank}(R)\geq K$.
The first literal charge follows from strict density.  For the second, a
nonzero same-sign petal vector has right-hand side $-Ca$ on the anchor.
Since $G=Q_{AA}^{-1}$ is strictly positive, $-GCa$ has no zero coordinate.
An explicitly returned row therefore has $n$ words.  The remaining charges
are distinct operations by the response-interface work contract.  The final
paragraph limits the claim to the exact decoder in
\cref{eq:notched-sun-linear-tag}.
\end{proof}

\begin{corollary}[The exact-rank stress test is easy at its separated finite
band]
\label{cor:notched-sun-finite-band-separation}
Fix $n\geq5$ and an additive-oracle parameter $\beta\in(0,1)$.  There is
$\overline\alpha_{n,\beta}\in[\alpha_n,1)$ such that the structural trace of
\cref{prop:notched-double-sun-loss-rank}, for every
$\alpha\in(\overline\alpha_{n,\beta},1)$, has the following property.  With
$\vartheta=(1-\alpha)/2$ and pruning threshold
\begin{equation}
 \tau_{\rm fb}:=\vartheta^5,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-finite-band-threshold}
\end{equation}
the $k$th exact loss increment obeys
\begin{equation}
 \left\{i:z_i^{(k)}\geq\tau_{\rm fb}\right\}=\{k\}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-single-heavy-loss}
\end{equation}
Moreover, for every report group $C\subseteq W$, the explicit scalar
\begin{equation}
 \widehat Z_k(C)
 :=\begin{cases}
    \vartheta^4/8+\beta\tau_{\rm fb},&w_k\in C,\\
    \beta\tau_{\rm fb},&w_k\notin C,
   \end{cases}
 \label{eq:notched-sun-separated-oracle}
\end{equation}
satisfies the certified one-sided additive bounds
\begin{equation}
 Z_k(C):=\sum_{w_i\in C}z_i^{(k)}
 \leq\widehat Z_k(C)
 \leq\frac{25}{4}Z_k(C)+\beta\tau_{\rm fb}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-separated-oracle-bound}
\end{equation}
Consequently the search of
\cref{cor:additive-group-locator} follows only the hierarchy path containing
$w_k$, uses $O(bL)$ group queries in an arity-$b$, depth-$L$ hierarchy, and
performs one exact leaf validation.  The path is identified from the exposed
matching incidences $f_k-u_k-w_k$; no dense loss update is published.
This is a query-count statement: constructing or updating the hierarchy and
its path-membership state, and the arithmetic cost of exact validation, remain
separate charged operations.

Thus the notched double sun is a sharp exact-representation obstruction but
cannot support a finite-band lower bound at
\cref{eq:notched-sun-finite-band-threshold} in this fixed-$n$,
$\alpha>\overline\alpha_{n,\beta}$ regime.  This remains a statement about the
prescribed structural trace, not an RPPR KKT trajectory or a uniform
fixed-$\alpha$ family as $n$ grows.
\end{corollary}

\begin{proof}
The asymptotics
\cref{eq:notched-sun-loss-diagonal,eq:notched-sun-loss-offdiagonal} hold for
the finite set of pairs $(i,k)$.  Hence, after increasing the lower bound on
$\alpha$ if necessary, simultaneously for every event,
\begin{equation}
 \frac{\vartheta^4}{50}
 \leq z_k^{(k)}\leq\frac{\vartheta^4}{8},
 \qquad
 \sum_{i\neq k}z_i^{(k)}\leq\beta\vartheta^5,
 \qquad
 \vartheta<\frac1{50}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-separated-scales}
\end{equation}
The first and third inequalities put the matching entry above
$\vartheta^5$, while the second puts every off-matching entry below it,
proving \cref{eq:notched-sun-single-heavy-loss}.

If $w_k\notin C$, then
$Z_k(C)\leq\beta\tau_{\rm fb}=\widehat Z_k(C)$, and the upper side of
\cref{eq:notched-sun-separated-oracle-bound} is immediate.  If $w_k\in C$,
then \cref{eq:notched-sun-separated-scales} gives
\begin{equation*}
 Z_k(C)\leq\frac{\vartheta^4}{8}+\beta\tau_{\rm fb}
 =\widehat Z_k(C)
 \leq\frac{25}{4}Z_k(C)+\beta\tau_{\rm fb}.
\end{equation*}
This proves the oracle bounds.  A group not containing $w_k$ has estimate
strictly below $\tau_{\rm fb}$ because $\beta<1$; a group containing $w_k$
has estimate above the threshold because $\vartheta<1/8$.  Therefore exactly
one branch survives at every level.  The graph construction exposes the
matched report label through the common anchor neighbor, proving the final
work statement.
\end{proof}

\begin{proposition}[Fresh explicit coded tables fail the epoch target]
\label{prop:notched-sun-fresh-dense-coded-table-charge}
Fix constants $\gamma>0$, $\beta,\delta\in(0,1)$, and
$\xi,\zeta\in(0,1/2)$.  For every $n\geq5$, let
$\overline\alpha_{n,\beta}$ be as in
\cref{cor:notched-sun-finite-band-separation}, and choose
\begin{equation}
 \alpha_n^\star\in
 \left(\max\{\overline\alpha_{n,\beta},3/4\},1\right).
 \label{eq:notched-sun-fresh-table-alpha-sequence}
\end{equation}
This is an $n$-dependent sequence; no fixed value of $\alpha$ is asserted to
work uniformly as $n$ grows.  Put
\begin{equation*}
 \vartheta_n:=\frac{1-\alpha_n^\star}{2},
 \qquad
 \tau_n:=\vartheta_n^5,
 \qquad
 K:=K_\gamma.
\end{equation*}
At event $1\leq k\leq K$, use the settled anchor, singleton frontier, and
remaining exterior
\begin{equation}
 A_k:=S_{k-1},
 \qquad F_k:=\{f_k\},
 \qquad U_k:=V\setminus A_k,
 \qquad
 \mathcal W_k:=W\mathbin{\dot\cup}\{f_{k+1},\ldots,f_n\}.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-fresh-table-event}
\end{equation}
Thus $U_k=F_k\mathbin{\dot\cup}\mathcal W_k$.  Use a global identifier
universe of size $N_n:=3n$, put
\begin{equation*}
 L_n:=\left\lceil\log_2N_n\right\rceil,
 \qquad M_n:=4n,
 \qquad
 \delta_k:=\frac{\delta}{k(k+1)},
\end{equation*}
and use the exact frontier representation, so that $\upsilon=0$ and
$a_\pm=(1\pm\zeta)^2$ in
\cref{eq:frontier-whitening-sandwich}.  Let $p_{q_n}$ be the corresponding
Chebyshev polynomial from
\cref{thm:chebyshev-frontier-whitening}.  For the public choice
$E_k:=1$, $m_k:=\sqrt{\tau_n}$, define
\begin{align}
 r_k
 &:=\left\lceil
      c_{\rm sk}\xi^{-2}\log\frac{4M_n}{\delta_k}
    \right\rceil,
 &
 R_k
 &:=\left\lceil\log_4\frac{2N_n}{\delta_k}\right\rceil,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-fresh-table-probe-counts}\\
 \mathsf h_n
 &:=\max\!\left\{1,
      \frac{(1+\xi)a_+}{(1-\xi)a_-\tau_n}
    \right\},
 &
 B_n
 &:=\left\lceil128\mathsf h_nL_n\right\rceil,
 \label{eq:notched-sun-fresh-table-buckets}\\
 \mathsf c_k&:=B_nR_k,
 &
 p_k&:=B_n(L_n+1)R_k=(L_n+1)\mathsf c_k.
 \label{eq:notched-sun-fresh-table-dimensions}
\end{align}

After the complete history has fixed
